Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on detection structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 675. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 349 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to detection structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 675/349 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in detection theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 572 and the invariant I(A,B) = 657 mod 5.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the detection decomposition. By minimality, the element a^572 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 657-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^5(S, F) is trivial. QED.
